# Skills · A · part 24

864 entries, agent-observability-spec to agent-registry. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **agent-observability-spec**: Specify the tracing, metrics, and alerting for an AI agent or LLM feature in production. Use when asked what to log for an LLM app, design agent tracing or spans, define quality and cost monitors, or answer 'how do we know if the agent is misbehaving?'. Produces an observability spec with a trace schema, metric definitions with owners and alert thresholds, sampling and retention policy, and a privacy note for logged content. ([mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills](https://github.com/mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pm-agentops/skills/agent-observability-spec) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **agent-observability-trace-rca**: Root cause analysis on production LLM traces. Diagnoses why an LLM application is failing — works from eval judge verdicts, runtime errors, or structural anomalies depending on what signals are present. Walks the span tree from symptom to root cause. Use when user says "what's wrong with my app", "why is my eval failing", "analyze errors", "root cause analysis", "diagnose failures", or wants to understand production failure patterns. ([datadog-labs/agent-skills](https://github.com/datadog-labs/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/agent-observability/agent-observability-trace-rca) · ★172 · 434 installs · 2 copies)
- **agent-observatory**: Answers questions about agent sessions across every project at once by reading the local observatory store: which sessions exist and are running, what each cost, which skills were used, and what failed. Where the harness allows, it can rename a session or message another one; it never starts, resumes, interrupts, or ends one. Use for "what sessions do I have running", "which agent is still working", "find the session that did X", "rename that session", "tell the other session about this", "what did that wave cost". Distinct from the observatory's own page, which reports and offers no action. ([hams-ollo/zen-agent-skills](https://github.com/hams-ollo/zen-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-observatory) · ★2)
- **agent-observer**: Use when the user asks which subagents ran, what model each subagent or task used, how a session was orchestrated, or wants a report of subagent activity.
